## `copy-paste`: Cloned sibling bodies that kept the wrong token

A function, struct, config block or asm sequence is cloned from a near neighbour. The copy keeps one token from the original that should have changed: the memory-kind table (DRAM offset array indexed by an L1 bank index), the core-flavour macro (active-eth MAP_END in an idle-eth map), the concrete template argument (multicast sub-command inside a body templated on the sub-command type), the forwarded callee (a `_with_fabric` binding that still calls the plain function), the union member written (a field of header A set inside the populate function for header B), a bit immediate (an LUI value copied from the neighbouring CSR routine, so it sets that routine's bit), or a loop variable (a container name one underscore away from the element name). A second shape is drift between N parallel bodies that must all do the same thing: one of four hand-written copy/move members skips a field, the write path of an address generator drops the non-power-of-two divisor and the per-bank offset that the read path has, a series term is duplicated both outside and inside the precise branch, or one physical size is passed through two symbols that are set in different scopes of one switch. Most of these fail silently because the leftover token still compiles and the wrong value is plausible: a default of 'replicate', a region that overlaps by only 2.5 KB, a bank that is valid, or a single-tile case that still passes.

**Checks**

- For every family of sibling functions or specializations (read/write/get_addr, DRAM/L1, unicast/multicast, active/idle eth, TRISC0..3, fused/non-fused), diff the bodies token by token. Every token that names a memory kind, core flavour, table, macro family, template type or union member must match the sibling's own identity. A token that belongs to the neighbour is the bug.
- In templated bodies, every inner call's explicit template argument and every sizeof() must be the template parameter or derived from it. A concrete type name that matches only one instantiation is a finding. Pair each size calculator with the writer it mirrors and check that both use the same element type in every instantiation.
- For classes with user-written copy/move ctors or assignment operators, list the non-static data members and confirm that every one of the four bodies transfers each member. Treat a body that is shorter than its siblings as suspect, and give priority to members whose default is a valid mode (replicate, interleaved, 0).
- For per-core memory maps, check that each region's base and end come from the same core-flavour macro family as its neighbours. Compute concrete addresses per arch to confirm the regions do not overlap, and confirm that a static or always-on check enforces this (a size bound on one ring alone is not enough).
- When one physical quantity reaches the toolchain or runtime through several symbols (stride and TLS size, ring size and next-region base), check that every variant sets all of them from one source in the same scope. Values set per case inside a switch next to values set once after it are the red flag.
- For bindings and wrappers, compare the exposed name with the callee and the documented return value. Flag X_with_Y that calls X, or a return tuple that differs from what the lambda actually returns.
- For union-backed headers and stateful set-once/update-masked APIs, check that each populate function writes only its own union member. Every field the consumer decodes must be written unconditionally at set_state time, not only on one branch of a flag such as flush.
- For hand-written register or CSR bit writes, decode the immediate into a bit index including the instruction's implicit scaling (LUI is <<12, slli N) and compare it with the bit the comment or function name promises. An encoding identical to a neighbouring routine with a different purpose is a copy.
- In unrolled series, Horner chains and repeated accumulate blocks, list each (power, coefficient) step across both the approximate and precise branches. Each term must appear exactly once. Float-to-int conversions used for range reduction must pass an explicit deterministic rounding mode.
- In loops or comprehensions where the loop variable differs from the container by a prefix or suffix (_x/x, item/items), check that every lookup in the body uses the element. For scalar-or-list config fields, check that the list length is validated against the number of consumers.

**Seeds** (code shapes to hunt for)

- grep -nE 'bank_to_dram_offset\[' in functions whose bank index comes from get_bank_index<false> or l1_* helpers; and '& \(NUM_(DRAM|L1)_BANKS ?- ?1\)' outside an is-power-of-two guard (tt_metal/hw/inc/internal/dataflow/)
- get_noc_addr_from_bank_id / get_*_noc_addr pairs: compare which per-kind offset (dram vs l1) each <DRAM>/<false> path adds (dataflow_api_addrgen.h)
- mem_map_bases[...UNRESERVED] / KERNEL_CONFIG lines that mix MEM_AERISC_* and MEM_IERISC_*, or a *_MAP_END from a different flavour than the file name (tt_metal/llrt/hal/tt-1xx/*, tt-2xx/quasar/*)
- template <typename T> bodies containing a concrete sibling type such as add_dispatch_write_packed<CQDispatchWritePackedMulticastSubCmd> or sizeof(CQDispatch...SubCmd) (tt_metal/impl/dispatch/)
- hand-written special members: grep -nE '\((const )?\w+ ?&&? ?other\)( noexcept)? ?(:|\{)' then compare the set of other.<member> uses per body with the class field list (tt_metal/impl, ttnn/core/tensor)
- populate_*_fields / *_with_state helpers in tt_metal/fabric/hw/inc/api_common.h that assign command_fields.<member not matching the function name>, or encoding/chunk fields set only under an `if (flag)` or one has_flag(Mask, ...) branch
- --defsym=__tls_size / __local_stride (or any paired size symbols) set in different scopes of one switch (tt_metal/llrt/hal/*/…_hal.cpp); linker '*(.tbss .tbss.*' / '*(.tdata .tdata.*' wildcards alongside start/end marker sections without EXCLUDE_FILE (tt_metal/hw/toolchain/*.ld)
- inline asm 'lui|li t[0-9], 0x..' followed by csrrs/csrrc on a custom CSR; decode the bit and compare it with the comment (tt_metal/hw/inc/internal/*firmware_common.h, risc_common.h)
- nanobind/pybind `.def("name", [](...){ ... self.other_name(` where other_name is a prefix of name; nested push_work(...push_work( (ttnn/cpp/ttnn-nanobind, distributed_nanobind.cpp)
- `for _X in X` / `for x in xs` whose body references the container (X.get(, xs[) instead of the element (tests/**/sweep*, python harnesses)

**Where it clusters:** tt_metal/hw/inc/internal/dataflow/ (address generators, DRAM vs L1 sibling paths); tt_metal/llrt/hal/ per-arch and per-core-type memory maps (active/idle eth, tensix, Quasar TRISC variants); tt_metal/fabric/hw/inc/api_common.h (union-backed packet header populate helpers, stateful masked APIs); tt_metal/impl/dispatch/ (templated command sizing vs writing, hand-written special members); tt_metal/hw/inc/internal firmware and toolchain (CSR asm immediates, linker scripts, link flags); SFPU ckernel headers (hand-unrolled series duplicated across approx/precise branches); ttnn tensor storage types and Python bindings (cloned wrappers, special members); tests sweep harness Python (loop/container name confusion)

**How fixes for this class went incomplete**

- A fix to one sibling path (for example the write-tile body) left the same drift possible in other hand-maintained variants. The durable fix was a refactor that routes every variant through one shared helper parameterised on the kind. Even after that, a free function outside the helper (get_l1_noc_addr) kept the copied DRAM table. Sweep every function in the family, not only the struct that failed.
- Fixing the mismatched token was not enough while nothing enforced the invariant. The memory-map fix needed an overlap assert called for every core type, and the stateful header fix needed a static_assert that Flush is in the set_state mask. Pair a copy-paste fix with a check that fails when the regions or fields diverge again.
- A fix for hand-written special members was reverted by CI and re-landed unchanged. Later code avoided the class of bug with `= default` plus a wrapped non-copyable member. Prefer eliminating the duplicated bodies over patching one of them.
- A superseding fix sometimes removes the wrong-variable bug but brings in a weaker sibling issue: positional list indexing with no length check, and per-entry labels and keys silently ignored. Re-audit the replacement for the same intent, not just for the original crash.
- A bug with two defects in one change (a doubled series term plus a nondeterministic rounding mode, or a duplicated size symbol plus a marker section swallowed by a linker wildcard) was only fully fixed because the analyst kept looking after the first defect. After finding one copy-paste token, re-read the whole cloned block.

**Candidate sites** (unverified leads)

- tt_metal/hw/inc/internal/dataflow/dataflow_api_addrgen.h:250 — get_l1_noc_addr computes an L1 bank_index but adds bank_to_dram_offset[bank_index]. This was copied from get_dram_noc_addr at line 236. The offset is wrong, and it reads out of bounds when the L1 bank index is >= NUM_DRAM_BANKS. No in-tree callers found, so the bug is latent.
- tt_metal/hw/inc/internal/dataflow/dataflow_api_addrgen.h:520 — get_noc_addr_from_bank_id<false> adds no per-bank offset for L1, while the shared get_bank_offset<false> helper (line 59) adds bank_to_l1_offset. Check whether omitting the L1 offset is intended.
- tt_metal/llrt/hal/tt-2xx/quasar/qa_hal_tensix.cpp:82 — the HAL LOCAL size is reported as the full MEM_TRISC_LOCAL_SIZE for every processor (the comment reads 'TRISC, BRISC, or NCRISC?'). The link flags give TRISC1/2 half that size, and DM uses its own local size. Any host consumer of this value may over-size the region.
- tt_metal/fabric/hw/inc/api_common.h:404 — under the WriteChunkSizes mask, the fused scatter+seminc helper sets the last chunk to packet_size_bytes - accumulated. packet_size_bytes defaults to 0 in *_with_state, so masking WriteChunkSizes without PayloadSize underflows. The non-fused scatter sibling was hardened for this case; this helper was not.
- tt_metal/impl/program/dispatch.cpp:2889 — the ring size for non-Tensix kernel-config buffers comes from the declared KERNEL_CONFIG size, not from the next region's base as on the Tensix path. It relies only on the HAL-init overlap assert, so a copied map-end macro elsewhere would again corrupt memory silently.
- tests/tt_eager/python_api_testing/sweep_tests/common.py:155 — the per-input datagen list is indexed by position with no length check against the number of inputs. The input_N labels are ignored, so a short list raises IndexError and a mis-ordered or long list silently applies the wrong generator.
